# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
Ransomware is a method that attackers use to spread a malware in a system and threat to delete or share secret data for a given ransom. 

- propagation would be e.g. phishing links or drive-by-downloads
- payload would be e.g. sql injection. ^a72pHrbM

if the user executes it through phishing or manipulated downloads 

Exploit in Browser/Office/Driver install payload without user interaction ^cVPFeknh

- Simple scanners (static): Where the anti-malware Software scans the system against known vulnerabilities. This approach is speedy and efficient but only works against known vulnerabilities (very limited)

- Heuristic scanners (static): this approach works by recognizing patterns in the code or by identifying suspicious Syscalls that might cause a threat (this is more efficient and finds more malware than the previous one). Ca. 60-95% detection rate

- Activity traps (dynamic): does not work based on patterns but rather on action, highly efficient but not as speedy as it requires computational power.  ^3pAG1uYi

A NULL DACL means that everyone has access to all objects

An empty DACL means no one has access at all. ^31ykBraw

- An Access-Token is set on login and remains fixed.
- The group membership changes only after the user logs in  again ^UOBFD2OH

Contacts -> {Instagram: RW, KakaoTalk: RW, Spotify: R}
Location -> {Instagram: R, KakaoTalk: R}
Music -> {KakaoTalk: R, Spotify: RW}
Pictures -> {Instagram: RW, KakaoTalk: RW} ^cU6Egd39

Instagram -> {Contacts: RW, Location: R, Pictures: RW}
KakaoTalk -> {Contacts: RW, Location: R, Music: R, Pictures: RW}
Spotify -> {Contacts: R, Music: RW}
 ^I9ymxOKc

In audit mode, WDAC logs every violation of the application whitelist without actually blocking execution. This allows, during the testing phase, to determine which applications and paths still need to be permitted before the whitelist is enforced in enforce mode. ^m5t3RCM7
